local function buildVignette(a1) -- Line: 445; upvalues: Config (val), u115 (val); types: a1: userdata
    local UIGradient, v1
    local LowHealthVignette = Config.Appearance.LowHealthVignette
    local v2 = Color3.fromRGB(LowHealthVignette.Color.r, LowHealthVignette.Color.g, LowHealthVignette.Color.b)
    local EdgeFraction = LowHealthVignette.EdgeFraction

    for i, j in {
        {
            rotation = 90,
            size = UDim2.fromScale(1, EdgeFraction),
            position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0),
        },
        {
            rotation = -90,
            size = UDim2.fromScale(1, EdgeFraction),
            position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1 - EdgeFraction),
        },
        {
            rotation = 0,
            size = UDim2.fromScale(EdgeFraction, 1),
            position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0),
        },
        {
            rotation = 180,
            size = UDim2.fromScale(EdgeFraction, 1),
            position = UDim2.fromScale(1 - EdgeFraction, 0),
        },
    } do
        v1 = Instance.new("Frame")
        v1.Name = "Vignette"
        v1.Size = j.size
        v1.Position = j.position
        v1.BackgroundColor3 = v2
        v1.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        v1.BorderSizePixel = 0
        v1.ZIndex = 0
        v1.Visible = false
        v1.Parent = a1
        UIGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        UIGradient.Rotation = j.rotation
        UIGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), (NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1))})
        UIGradient.Parent = v1

        table.insert(u115, v1)
    end
end

local function backpackKey()

    end

        if BuildController.closeIfOpen(j) then
            return
        end
    end

end

local function aimedFlowerId() -- Line: 942; upvalues: Aim (val), LocalPlayer (val), Config (val), Grid (val)
    local Attribute
    local v1 = Aim.ray()
    local v2 = RaycastParams.new()
    v2.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    v2.FilterDescendantsInstances = if not LocalPlayer.Character then {} else {LocalPlayer.Character}
    local v3 = workspace:Raycast(v1.Origin, v1.Direction.Unit * Config.Player.BuildReachCells * Grid.CellSize, v2)
    local Instance = v3 and v3.Instance

    while Instance do
        if Instance == workspace then
            break
        end

        Attribute = Instance:GetAttribute("FlowerId")

        if type(Attribute) == "string" then
            return Attribute
        end

        Instance = Instance.Parent
    end

    return nil
end

local function cycleHotbar(a1) -- Line: 961; upvalues: Config (val), HotbarController (val); types: a1: number

        v1 = (v1 - 1 + a1) % Slots + 1

        if HotbarController.isSlotShown(v1) then
            HotbarController.select(v1)
            return
        end
    end
end

        if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft then
            if not PortalMoveController.isActive() then
                BuildController.rotate()
            else
                PortalMoveController.rotate()
            end

            return true
        end

        if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR3 and Aim.isVR() then
            if not PortalMoveController.isActive() then
                BuildController.rotate()
            else
                PortalMoveController.rotate()
            end

            return true
        end

        if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2 then
            if not BuildController.isUiOpen() then
                BuildController.flip()
            end

            return true
        end

        if a1 == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL3 then
            u178.toggleSprintButton()
            return true
        end

        if a1 ~= Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB then
            return false
        end

        if PortalMoveController.isActive() then
            PortalMoveController.cancel()
            return true
        end

        closeOpenPanels()

        return true
    end

    local v1 = if a1 ~= Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1 then -1 else 1

    if not BuildController.isUiOpen() or not PanelTabs.step(v1) then
        cycleHotbar(v1)
    end

    return true
end

    if CooldownDays >= 2 then
        return (("%* days"):format((math.floor(CooldownDays + 0.5))))
    end

    local v1 = CooldownDays * 24

    if v1 >= 1 then
        return (("%* hours"):format((math.floor(v1 + 0.5))))
    end

    v1 = CooldownDays * 24 * 60

    if v1 >= 1 then
        return (("%* minutes"):format((math.floor(v1 + 0.5))))
    end

    return (("%* seconds"):format((math.floor(v1 * 60 + 0.5))))
end

local function findPortal() -- Line: 29; upvalues: Config (val)

    local v2 = workspace:FindFirstChild(Config.Hub.FolderName)

    if not (if not v2 then nil else v2:FindFirstChild(Config.Hub.Ruins.PortalName)) then
        return nil, (Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
    end

    if v1:IsA("BasePart") then
        return v1, (Vector3.new(0, v1.Size.Y / 2, 0))
    end

    if not v1:IsA("Model") then
        return nil, (Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
    end

    local PrimaryPart = v1.PrimaryPart or v1:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)

    if not PrimaryPart then
        return nil, (Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
    end

    local BoundingBox, BoundingBox_2 = v1:GetBoundingBox()

    return PrimaryPart,
        BoundingBox.Position + Vector3.new(0, BoundingBox_2.Y / 2, 0)
            - PrimaryPart.Position
end

local function aimed(a1, a2) -- Line: 169; upvalues: BallistaArt (val); types: a1: table, a2: vector
    local Pivot = a1.art:GetPivot()
    local v1 = Pivot:ToObjectSpace(a1.home)
    local v2 = Pivot:VectorToObjectSpace(a2 - a1.home.Position)

    if v2.Magnitude < 0.1 then
        return a1.home
    end

    local Unit = v2.Unit
    local v3 = math.atan2(-Unit.X, -Unit.Z)
    local v4 = math.clamp(math.asin((math.clamp(Unit.Y, -1, 1))), math.rad(BallistaArt.MinPitch), (math.rad(BallistaArt.MaxPitch)))

    return Pivot * CFrame.new(v1.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, v3, 0)
        * CFrame.Angles(v4, 0, 0)
        * v1.Rotation
end

local function turnTo(a1, a2, a3, a4)
    -- Line: 187
    -- upvalues: RunService (val)
    -- types: a1: table, a2: userdata, a3: number, a4: function?

    local Pivot = a1.model:GetPivot()
    local u12 = os.clock()
    local u13 = nil
    u13 = (RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        -- Line: 193
        -- upvalues: a1 (val), turn (val), u13 (ref), a3 (val), u12 (val), Pivot (val), a2 (val), a4 (val)
        if a1.turn == turn and a1.model.Parent then
            local v1
            a1.model:PivotTo((Pivot:Lerp(a2, if not (a3 > 0) then 1 else math.clamp((os.clock() - u12) / a3, 0, 1))))

            if v1 >= 1 then
                if u13 then
                    u13:Disconnect()
                end

                if a4 then

                end
            end

            return
        end

        end
    end))
end

local function flyRailBolt(a1, a2)
    -- Line: 214
    -- upvalues: drawBolt (val), trail (val), BoltSpeed (val), BoltMaxSeconds (val), TweenService (val), impact (val)
    -- upvalues: landed (val), BallistaArt (val)
    -- types: a1: table, a2: vector
    local bolt = a1.bolt

    if not bolt then
        drawBolt(a1.model:GetPivot().Position, a2)
        return
    end

    local u89 = bolt:Clone()

    for i, j in u89:GetDescendants() do
        if j:IsA("JointInstance") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint") or j:IsA("Constraint") then
            j:Destroy()
        end
    end

    u89.Anchored = true
    u89.CanCollide = false
    u89.CanQuery = false
    u89.CanTouch = false

    trail(u89)

    u89.Parent = workspace
    local CFrame_2 = bolt.CFrame
    local v1 = a2 - CFrame_2.Position
    local v2 = math.min(v1.Magnitude / BoltSpeed, BoltMaxSeconds)
    local v3 = TweenService:Create(
        u89,
        TweenInfo.new(v2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
        {CFrame = CFrame_2 + v1}
    )

    v3.Completed:Connect(function() -- Line: 240; upvalues: impact (upval), a2 (val), landed (upval), u89 (val)
        impact(a2)
        landed(u89)
    end)

    v3:Play()

    bolt.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
    task.delay(
        BallistaArt.ReloadSeconds,
        function() -- Line: 246; upvalues: bolt (val)
            bolt.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
        end
    )
end

local function shoot(a1, a2, a3)
    -- Line: 253
    -- upvalues: SlimeFarm (val), turretOf (val), drawBolt (val), turnTo (val), aimed (val), BallistaArt (val)
    -- upvalues: flyRailBolt (val)
    -- types: a1: userdata, a2: vector, a3: vector
    local CurrentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    if CurrentCamera
        and SlimeFarm.ShotDrawStuds
            < (CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position - a1.Position).Magnitude then
        return
    end

    local u15 = turretOf(a1)

    if not u15 then
        drawBolt(a2, a3)
        return
    end

    local u23 = os.clock()
    u15.lastShotAt = u23

    turnTo(
        u15,
        aimed(u15, a3),
        BallistaArt.TurnSeconds,
        function() -- Line: 265; upvalues: flyRailBolt (upval), u15 (val), a3 (val)
            flyRailBolt(u15, a3)
        end
    )

    task.delay(
        BallistaArt.IdleSeconds,
        function() -- Line: 268; upvalues: u15 (val), u23 (val), turnTo (upval)
            if u15.lastShotAt == u23 then
                turnTo(u15, u15.home, 0.6, nil)
            end
        end
    )
end
